URBAN DEVELOPMENT DEPARTMENT
Mantralaya, Mumbai 400 032,
dated the 24th April, 2024.
NOTIFICATION
No. SLB-2025/C.R.18/UD-4.— Service level regarding water supply, sewerage disposal, storm
water management and solid waste management expected to be achieved by the 31st March 2025 for all
urban local bodies in the State of Maharashtra are notified in the Official Gazette vide this notification.

By order and in the name of the Governor of Maharashtra,
SUSHILA PAWAR,
Deputy Secretary to Government.

15th Finance Commission, Government of India
